You are reviewing one branch in this repository (La Hoja Boricua). Review only; do not edit files. Do not load or run any skills. The worktree is C:/Users/Owner/lhb-47-wt (branch lhb-47-ai-baseline). This is round 2. The branch adds files under evidence/ only.

Round 1 (evidence/lhb-47-review-r1.txt) blocked on two findings:
1. evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots.py took the top 1000 user agents of ALL traffic before filtering for crawlers, so a low-volume crawler could be silently dropped.
2. evidence/lhb-47-ai-visibility-2026-10.txt said the baseline "predates any crawl" of the new pages, which nothing measured.

The fixes:
1. The crawler filter now runs in the query: filter is a variable of type ZoneHttpRequestsAdaptiveGroupsFilter_InputObject with an OR of userAgent_like '%<bot>%' terms, so the limit (now 10000) applies to crawler user agents only; a result that reaches the limit is refused with exit 3 and no counts.
2. The baseline now says the crawl timing is NOT VERIFIED.
3. evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots-run-r1fix.txt: the fixed script run against the live API reaches the authorization check (the token lacks Analytics Read), while two controls with a deliberately broken argument (a misspelled filter argument; a misspelled field inside OR) fail earlier, at argument parsing. That is offered as evidence the query and its filter validate.

Check: is either round 1 defect still present? Is the parsing-before-authorization argument sound? Can the new script undercount, overcount (a user agent matching two bots), crash on a valid response, or leak the token? Does the baseline state anything it cannot support?

Report each finding with file, symbol, a concrete failing scenario, and whether it blocks. End with exactly one line: VERDICT: PASS or VERDICT: BLOCK (BLOCK only for a defect that should be fixed before merge).

TOOLS: if your shell fails to start (helper_unknown_error has happened here), use your Node tool (node_repl) with require("fs") and require("child_process") to read files and run commands in the worktree, for example the suite: node --test --test-timeout=60000 test/site.test.js. If no tool works at all, review from the diff embedded below and say which claims you could not run.

+++ b/evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots.py
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+"""Count requests from known AI crawlers on lahojaboricua.com through the Cloudflare GraphQL Analytics API.
+Reads CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN from the environment; prints the counts or the API's own error. Never prints the token.
+The crawler filter runs in the query (OR of userAgent_like), so the row limit applies to crawler user agents only; a
+result that reaches the limit is refused (exit 3) rather than reported, because it may be cut short.
+usage: python lhb-47-ai-bots.py DAYS"""
+import json, os, sys, urllib.request, datetime
+ZONE = '25ebca85f3121058137e3ea8b5a65c43'
+LIMIT = 10000
+days = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 7
+end = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0)
+start = end - datetime.timedelta(days=days)
+BOTS = ['GPTBot', 'ChatGPT-User', 'OAI-SearchBot', 'ClaudeBot', 'Claude-User', 'Claude-SearchBot', 'PerplexityBot', 'Perplexity-User',
+        'Google-Extended', 'Applebot', 'Bytespider', 'CCBot', 'Amazonbot', 'meta-externalagent', 'DuckAssistBot', 'MistralAI-User']
+q = '''query($zone:String!,$filter:ZoneHttpRequestsAdaptiveGroupsFilter_InputObject!){viewer{zones(filter:{zoneTag:$zone}){
+  httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(limit:%d,filter:$filter,orderBy:[count_DESC]){count dimensions{userAgent}}}}}''' % LIMIT
+flt = {'datetime_geq': start.isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z'), 'datetime_lt': end.isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z'),
+       'requestSource': 'eyeball', 'OR': [{'userAgent_like': '%' + b + '%'} for b in BOTS]}
+body = json.dumps({'query': q, 'variables': {'zone': ZONE, 'filter': flt}}).encode()
+req = urllib.request.Request('https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/graphql', body, {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + os.environ['CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN'], 'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+d = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
+if d.get('errors'): print('errors:', json.dumps(d['errors'])[:600]); sys.exit(1)
+rows = d['data']['viewer']['zones'][0]['httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups']
+if len(rows) >= LIMIT:
+    print('INCOMPLETE: %d crawler user-agent groups returned, the limit; counts not reported. Shorten DAYS.' % len(rows)); sys.exit(3)
+tot = {b: 0 for b in BOTS}
+for r in rows:
+    ua = (r['dimensions']['userAgent'] or '').lower()
+    for b in BOTS:  # a user agent naming two crawlers counts once, under the first in BOTS
+        if b.lower() in ua: tot[b] += r['count']; break
+print('window', start.isoformat(), 'to', end.isoformat(), '| crawler user-agent groups read', len(rows), '| requests in those groups', sum(r['count'] for r in rows))
+for b in BOTS: print('%-20s %d' % (b, tot[b]))
+print('AI crawlers total', sum(tot.values()))

+LHB-47 AI and search visibility baseline, 2026-10 (first reading)
+
+Taken 2026-10-28T13:27Z (09:27 UTC-4).
+
+Counting basis: 20 fixed questions (10 Spanish, 10 English), one engine (the Claude Code WebSearch tool, standard mode),
+one run per question, top results as returned (about 10 per question). A question counts as a HIT when any returned
+result URL is on lahojaboricua.com or the result text cites La Hoja Boricua the site; a mention of a cigar brand named
+"Hoja Boricua" is recorded separately as a NAME MENTION, not a hit.
+
+Result: 0 of 20 questions returned lahojaboricua.com. 0 of 20 cited the site. 2 of 20 carried a NAME MENTION of a
+different "Hoja Boricua" (a brand or trademark, not this site).
+
+ #  question                                         hit  note
+ 1  mejores cigarros puertorriqueños                 no
+ 2  dónde fumar cigarros en San Juan                 no
+ 3  cigar lounge en Viejo San Juan                   no
+ 4  artesanos de cigarros en Puerto Rico             no
+ 5  cigarros hechos en Puerto Rico                   no
+ 6  tabaquerías en Puerto Rico                       no
+ 7  dónde comprar cigarros en Caguas                 no
+ 8  torcedores de tabaco Puerto Rico                 no
+ 9  cigar lounge Ponce Puerto Rico                   no
+10  la ruta del tabaco Puerto Rico                   no
+11  where to smoke cigars in Old San Juan            no
+12  best cigar lounges in Puerto Rico                no
+13  Puerto Rican handmade cigars                     no
+14  cigar shops in San Juan Puerto Rico              no
+15  cigar makers in Puerto Rico                      no
+16  are cigars made in Puerto Rico                   no

+20  La Hoja Boricua cigars                           no   NAME MENTION: HOJA BORICUA trademark of JFC Tobacco Corp.
+
+Sources that recur across the 20 result sets (the pages an answer engine currently draws on for this topic):
+The Cigar House, Frommer's, Wanderlog, Discover Puerto Rico, NBC News, Club Macanudo.
+
+AI crawler requests: NOT MEASURED. The Cloudflare GraphQL Analytics query (evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots.py) was refused
+because the API token lacks the zone Analytics Read permission. Not worked around; changing token permissions is the
+owner's. When the permission exists: CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN=... python evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots.py 30
+
+Context at this reading: the English twin pages (LHB-32) and the 22 guide pages (LHB-45) went live the same morning,
+hours before these searches. Whether any crawler had fetched them by 13:27Z is NOT VERIFIED (crawler requests were not
+measured, above). Sitemap resubmission waits on LHB-6 (owner gate).
+
+Re-check: monthly, the same 20 questions in the same order, written to evidence/lhb-47-ai-visibility-YYYY-MM.txt.
+Scheduled as a dated Linear ticket each month (the first: LHB-91, the 2026-11 reading, due 2026-11-30), not as a clock-run
+session, per the owner's ruling "no time-based executions, resource-based only" (pre-contracts 3e7d497). Each
+reading's ticket files the next month's before it closes.

+You are reviewing one branch in this repository (La Hoja Boricua). Review only; do not edit files. Do not load or run any skills. The worktree is C:/Users/Owner/lhb-47-wt (branch lhb-47-ai-baseline). This is round 2. The branch adds files under evidence/ only.
+
+Round 1 (evidence/lhb-47-review-r1.txt) blocked on two findings:
+1. evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots.py took the top 1000 user agents of ALL traffic before filtering for crawlers, so a low-volume crawler could be silently dropped.
+2. evidence/lhb-47-ai-visibility-2026-10.txt said the baseline "predates any crawl" of the new pages, which nothing measured.
+
+The fixes:
+1. The crawler filter now runs in the query: filter is a variable of type ZoneHttpRequestsAdaptiveGroupsFilter_InputObject with an OR of userAgent_like '%<bot>%' terms, so the limit (now 10000) applies to crawler user agents only; a result that reaches the limit is refused with exit 3 and no counts.
+2. The baseline now says the crawl timing is NOT VERIFIED.
+3. evidence/lhb-47-ai-bots-run-r1fix.txt: the fixed script run against the live API reaches the authorization check (the token lacks Analytics Read), while two controls with a deliberately broken argument (a misspelled filter argument; a misspelled field inside OR) fail earlier, at argument parsing. That is offered as evidence the query and its filter validate.
+
+Check: is either round 1 defect still present? Is the parsing-before-authorization argument sound? Can the new script undercount, overcount (a user agent matching two bots), crash on a valid response, or leak the token? Does the baseline state anything it cannot support?
+
+Report each finding with file, symbol, a concrete failing scenario, and whether it blocks. End with exactly one line: VERDICT: PASS or VERDICT: BLOCK (BLOCK only for a defect that should be fixed before merge).

++"""Count requests from known AI crawlers on lahojaboricua.com through the Cloudflare GraphQL Analytics API.
++Reads CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN from the environment; prints the counts or the API's own error. Never prints the token.
++usage: python aibots.py DAYS"""
++import json, os, sys, urllib.request, datetime
++ZONE = '25ebca85f3121058137e3ea8b5a65c43'
++days = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 7
++end = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0)
++start = end - datetime.timedelta(days=days)
++BOTS = ['GPTBot', 'ChatGPT-User', 'OAI-SearchBot', 'ClaudeBot', 'Claude-User', 'Claude-SearchBot', 'PerplexityBot', 'Perplexity-User',
++        'Google-Extended', 'Applebot', 'Bytespider', 'CCBot', 'Amazonbot', 'meta-externalagent', 'DuckAssistBot', 'MistralAI-User']
++q = '''query($zone:String!,$start:Time!,$end:Time!){viewer{zones(filter:{zoneTag:$zone}){
++  httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(limit:1000,filter:{datetime_geq:$start,datetime_lt:$end,requestSource:"eyeball"},orderBy:[count_DESC]){count dimensions{userAgent}}}}}'''
++body = json.dumps({'query': q, 'variables': {'zone': ZONE, 'start': start.isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z'), 'end': end.isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z')}}).encode()
++req = urllib.request.Request('https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/graphql', body, {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + os.environ['CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN'], 'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
++d = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
++if d.get('errors'): print('errors:', json.dumps(d['errors'])[:600]); sys.exit(1)
++rows = d['data']['viewer']['zones'][0]['httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups']
++tot = {b: 0 for b in BOTS}
++for r in rows:
++    ua = r['dimensions']['userAgent'] or ''
++    for b in BOTS:
++        if b.lower() in ua.lower(): tot[b] += r['count']; break
++print('window', start.isoformat(), 'to', end.isoformat(), '| user-agent groups read', len(rows), '| all requests in those groups', sum(r['count'] for r in rows))
++for b in BOTS: print('%-20s %d' % (b, tot[b]))
++print('AI crawlers total', sum(tot.values()))

++result URL is on lahojaboricua.com or the result text cites La Hoja Boricua the site; a mention of a cigar brand named
++"Hoja Boricua" is recorded separately as a NAME MENTION, not a hit.
